it before editing. A path claim prevents accidental agent races; it is not a
claim of permanent subsystem ownership.

 **DONE 2026-09-23 (Batch 6 of the oldest-partial queue — Plans 135 + 59, host integration):** (1) `Plan 135` — weather is no longer a number the UI prints. `WeatherCascadeSeverity` (Core, no mutable state) derives a front's severity entirely from the canonical `WeatherEffectsCatalog` — the same table `WeatherSystem` already consumes for rad/visibility/thermal/travel — across five named axes normalized to 0..1 against named reference constants and combined with integer-permille weights summing to exactly 1000, so the cascade can never disagree with the weather the player is standing in and an unbound catalog yields 0 (no cascade) rather than a fabricated value. `WeatherCascadeHostSession` binds the authored 15-row `weather_gameplay_effects.json` table through a **new strict loader** (`WeatherCascadeCatalogLoader` rejects an unknown weather_kind, an unknown target_system/effect_type, a non-finite magnitude, a sub-day duration, a duplicate id, and an empty table) via a new `Engine.BindValidatedTemplates` seam, which makes the engine's lenient generated-effect fallback unreachable from the host. Each produced effect then reaches **its canonical owner** and only that owner: shelter `damage` → `DisasterResponseSystem.AdjustResilience`; shelter `thermal_load`/`filtration_stress`/`power_output` → **reported only**, because they already reach the shelter through the canonical weather path and applying them again would be a second drain (proven by a dedicated check); expedition `delay`/`damage`/`hazard_surge` → `ExpeditionSystem.SetEncounterChanceMultiplier` (the owner still owns the roll); economy `price_change` → `MarketSystem.ApplyShock` with its own clamp and idempotence, named `weather_<effectId>` so the source is auditable; mental-health `behavior_change` → `NeedsSystem.SetExternalModifier` bounded to the effect's duration (no morale write, no fabricated crisis case — the mental-health owner has no probability seam); location `accessibility` → **reported only**, because `RouteAvailabilityKind` is documented as derived-and-never-serialized. Fortification is read from the canonical resilience owner, never stored. The session's day owner (phase 5, `IPreDaySnapshotRestore`) expires fronts and withdraws every modifier it installed, the state persists through the new registered `weather_cascade` section, and the canonical `OnWeatherChanged` hook fires one cascade per (kind, day). 35-check `--weather-cascade-selftest`. (2) `Plan 59` — the retrospective's own gate made real. `StandingGatesHostSession` loads the 22-row register through a **new strict loader** that never defaults a tier, never drops an unowned gate, and rejects a gate that declares no scripted enforcement and no written decision, then measures each rule against the repo's **actual enforcement authority** (`docs/ci/CI_GATE_MANIFEST.json`, 57 gates) with five verdicts — `Enforced` (real, critical, tier-compatible gate), `RuleOnly` (deliberately not scripted, with owner and dated written reason), `Unbound`, `NotBlocking` (not critical, so a red does not block), `TierMismatch`. Every one of the 22 rows has an owner; 19 bind a real critical CI gate; 3 carry explicit 2026-09-23 written decisions (mass-balance soak, reproducible balance sweeps, AI-disclosure provenance). Tier semantics are exact: a per-push rule requires a `fast` gate, nightly/per-release accept either, so a `full` soak does not pass as per-push. Self-proof fixtures prove the register's own gates can fail (ownerless, empty, unknown tier, unwritten decision, bogus enforcement ref, tier override). 19-check `--standing-gates-selftest`. Blocked-and-resolved in-batch: the lenient `LoadCatalog` fallback (invented cascade), the unguarded state schema, a neutral front still firing a generated `default_morale_lift` (found by the probe), the blizzard-vs-BlackRain damage routing (the probe asserted the wrong front), an over-strict tier rule that flagged 8 correctly enforced gates, one genuine mismatch (`gate_vocabulary_contract_events` per_push → `test_core_suite` full tier) resolved by retiering the rule to the cadence its gate actually runs at rather than weakening the check, `CatalogIntegrityValidator` resolving `enforcement_ref` as a game-data id, an unclassified day-event kind, an untracked Core `Bind*` seam, and a missing architecture-map node. Evidence: host + Core test builds 0/0 warnings; `--weather-cascade-selftest` 35/35; `--standing-gates-selftest` 19/19; `--data-integrity-selftest` PASS (0 errors, 420 catalogs); `--port-contract-selftest` PASS 287 seams / 183 host-required / 9-of-9 host wiring; `--selftest-manifest` PASS; `--7-day-smoke-selftest` PASS; `--save-store-checksum-selftest` PASS; `--retention-selftest` 18/18 and `--outpost-settlement-selftest` 28/28 (Batch 5 regression); `Governance` 48/48; `Weather` 27/27; `Save` 1453/1453; `Records` 13/13; `Settlements` 12/12; `Expeditions` 331/331; `Tooling` 120/122; `Campaign` 256/257; `MainTriadDriftGateTests` 7/7; `PersistentFilenameRegistryGateTests` 4/4; `LoaderWiringGateTests` 4/4; `CampaignRngSourceGateTests` 2/2; `SelfTestManifestGateTests` 4/4; section pin 222→223; all five generators in sync; `agent-fast-verify` 10/10. Pre-existing failures left untouched (none inside this claim): `Plan31BriefingRouteTests.GenericNonHeartbeatEvent_BecomesActionable` (D11-B, decision-blocked), `CaseFoldPolicyPinTests` band (400 vs [120,260]), `CatalogPathForbiddenGateTests` on `src/UI/FactionCultureCodexPanel.cs`, and `--real-campaign-journey-selftest` (isolated by a causation test: with this claim's entire weather-cascade trigger disabled and rebuilt, the identical two failures and the identical two victim ids reproduce, and the `Combat/TacticalCombatSystem*.cs` files the log names are dirty in the shared worktree from concurrent work — 852 modified files at audit time). Deferred with named reasons in the readiness plan §6: RNG shakes, the weather mini-game, weather quests/legacy/UI panel, the `VentilationSystem`/`SumpFloodingSystem` second couplings, faction-operation pause (no standing-operation ledger exists), mental-health crisis probability (no probability seam), Plan 59A's CI-authoring and doc-generation substeps (7 of 13), and Plan 59B/59C entirely (a governance-document package, not a host-integration package). The audit report is persisted through the registered `retention` section, and a 400-day pass is idempotent (nothing more moves once bounded). Hostile pressure uses the campaign's own forked `outpost_risk` stream (one draw per day per outpost), starvation/overrun consequences journal through the canonical journal owner and `CampaignConsequenceLedger`, the state persists through the new `outpost_settlement` section, and the day owner ticks in phase 5 with a pre-day snapshot rollback. The 28-check `--outpost-settlement-selftest` proves authored-load, canonical build-cost billing, insufficient-stock refusal, bunk/fitness/duplicate gates, canonical ration draw, real starvation and recovery, a 30-day deterministic replay, at-most-once overrun, field-exact capture/restore, and phantom-row rejection. ## Unregistered worktree safety

The repository already contains substantial user and other-agent changes that
predate this batch. They are not available for opportunistic cleanup, rebasing,
formatting, or conflict resolution. A package may touch only its listed paths.

## Claim lifecycle

- `ACTIVE`: owner may edit exact paths.
- `HANDED_OFF`: owner stopped; integrator or named successor may proceed.
- `BLOCKED`: no edits until foreman records a new decision.
- `ACCEPTED` / `REVOKED`: claim is closed and paths become available.

Claims name exact files or narrow directories. Shared composition roots,
registries, and generated authority files belong to the integrator, never to
parallel builders.
